## 濠电姷鏁搁崑娑㈡嚐椤栨熬鑰块梺顒€绉撮弸浣肝旈敐鍛殲闁稿顑囬惀顏堫敇閻愭潙娅ｇ紓浣瑰姈閻╊垶寮婚妶澶婄閹艰揪绲块悡鈧柣搴ゎ潐濞叉粓宕伴弽顓炴瀬鐎广儱顦柋鍥ㄧ節婵犲倹顥戦柟灏佸墲娣囧﹪鎮欓鍕ㄥ亾濡ゅ懌鈧倿顢欓悙顒夋綗?110 婵犵數鍋為崹鍫曞箰閹间礁鐤い鎰剁畱妗呴梺鎼炲労閸撴岸宕戠€ｎ剛妫い鎾跺仦閸ｅ湱绱掗幇顓犳创闁?111 婵犵數鍋為崹鍫曞箰閹间絸鍥ㄥ閺夋嚦銉╂煕鐏炴儳鍤柛銈呯墦閹綊宕崟顒佸創闁诲函绲洪崑鎾斥攽閻愭潙鐏﹂柟鍛婂劤閳诲秹濮€閵忊槅娼熼柡澶婄墐閺呮繈鎯屾径濞掑綊鏁愰崨顓ф闂佺粯鎸搁妶鎼佸箖瀹勬壋鏋庨幖娣焺濡倿鏌ｆ惔銊︽锭闁活厼鍊搁悾宄邦潩椤戔晜妫冨畷鐔煎煘閹傚闂佸憡鍔忛弲婵嬨€呴弻銉︾厵闁绘垶锚閻忋儳鎮悢鍏尖拺?

skonester_cheat_menu.0110 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0110.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = learning



  right_portrait = scope:skonester_trait_edit_target
  immediate = {
    skonester_cheat_menu_sync_trait_edit_target_effect = yes
  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_immortal

    custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_immortal_tt
    scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = {

      if = { limit = { has_trait = skonester_trait_immortal_ageless } remove_trait = skonester_trait_immortal_ageless }

      else = {

        set_age = 25

        if = { limit = { has_trait = incapable } remove_trait = incapable }

        skonester_cheat_menu_grant_cheat_trait_effect = { TRAIT = skonester_trait_immortal_ageless }

      }

    }


    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0110 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_warlord

    custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_warlord_tt
    scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = { if = { limit = { has_trait = trait_warlord_eternal } remove_trait = trait_warlord_eternal } else = { skonester_cheat_menu_grant_cheat_trait_effect = { TRAIT = trait_warlord_eternal } } }


    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0110 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_wealth

    custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_wealth_tt
    scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = { if = { limit = { has_trait = trait_wealth_sovereign_max } remove_trait = trait_wealth_sovereign_max } else = { skonester_cheat_menu_grant_cheat_trait_effect = { TRAIT = trait_wealth_sovereign_max } } }


    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0110 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_intrigue

    custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_intrigue_tt
    scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = { if = { limit = { has_trait = trait_ultimate_intrigue_master } remove_trait = trait_ultimate_intrigue_master } else = { skonester_cheat_menu_grant_cheat_trait_effect = { TRAIT = trait_ultimate_intrigue_master } } }


    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0110 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_great_builder

    custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_great_builder_tt
    scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = { if = { limit = { has_trait = trait_great_builder_god } remove_trait = trait_great_builder_god } else = { skonester_cheat_menu_grant_cheat_trait_effect = { TRAIT = trait_great_builder_god } } }


    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0110 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_nether_lord

    custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_nether_lord_tt
    scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = { if = { limit = { has_trait = trait_nether_conspiracy_lord } remove_trait = trait_nether_conspiracy_lord } else = { skonester_cheat_menu_grant_cheat_trait_effect = { TRAIT = trait_nether_conspiracy_lord } } }


    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0110 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_eternal_nether

    custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_eternal_nether_tt
    scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = { if = { limit = { has_trait = trait_eternal_nether_lord } remove_trait = trait_eternal_nether_lord } else = { skonester_cheat_menu_grant_cheat_trait_effect = { TRAIT = trait_eternal_nether_lord } } }


    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0110 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0110.page_next

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0111 }

  }



  option = { name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.close }

}



skonester_cheat_menu.0096 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0096.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = diplomacy



  immediate = {

    skonester_cheat_menu_sync_relation_target_effect = yes

  }



  right_portrait = scope:skonester_relation_target



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0096.best_friend

    trigger = {

      NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled }

      exists = scope:skonester_relation_target

      NOT = { scope:actor = scope:skonester_relation_target }

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_relation_confirm_best_friend_effect = yes

    trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0096

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0096.lover

    trigger = { NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled } }

    skonester_cheat_menu_relation_confirm_lover_effect = yes

    trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0096

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0096.soulmate

    trigger = {

      NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled }

      exists = scope:skonester_relation_target

      NOT = { scope:actor = scope:skonester_relation_target }

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_relation_confirm_soulmate_effect = yes

    trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0096

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0096.nemesis

    trigger = {

      NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled }

      exists = scope:skonester_relation_target

      NOT = { scope:actor = scope:skonester_relation_target }

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_relation_confirm_nemesis_effect = yes

    trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0096

  }



  option = { name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.close }

}



skonester_cheat_menu.0098 = {

  type = character_event

  hidden = yes



  immediate = {



    if = {
      limit = { NOT = { has_variable = skonester_heir_ui_refresh_counter } }
      set_variable = { name = skonester_heir_ui_refresh_counter value = 1 }
    }
    else = {
      change_variable = { name = skonester_heir_ui_refresh_counter add = 1 }
    }

  }

}



skonester_cheat_menu.0099 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0099.t

  desc = skonester_cheat_menu.0099.desc

  theme = diplomacy

  immediate = {
    skonester_cheat_menu_sync_switch_target_effect = yes
  }

  right_portrait = scope:skonester_switch_target

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0099.confirm

    add_internal_flag = special

    trigger = {
      exists = scope:skonester_switch_target
      scope:skonester_switch_target = {
        is_alive = yes
        is_playable_character = yes
        is_ai = yes
        NOT = { this = root }
      }
    }

    skonester_switch_playable_character_effect = yes

    skonester_cheat_menu_clear_recipient_effect = yes

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0099.cancel

  }

}



## 濠电姷鏁搁崑娑㈡嚐椤栨熬鑰块梺顒€绉撮弸浣肝旈敐鍛殲闁稿顑囬惀顏堫敇閻愭潙娅ｇ紓浣瑰姈閻╊垶寮婚妶澶婄閹艰揪绲块悡鈧柣搴ゎ潐濞叉粓宕伴弽顓炴瀬鐎广儱顦柋鍥煏韫囧﹥娅呴柛鎾规珪缁绘稓鈧稒顭囬惌濠囨煟椤撶偛鈧寧淇婄€涙ê绶為柟閭﹀幗濞呮牕鈹戦鏂や緵闁稿妫濋幃鐤疀閺傚墽绠氬┑掳鍊曢鍥礆閻楀牏绠鹃柛鈩冨姇閻忔煡鏌ｅ☉鍗炴灓缂佽鲸鐟╅幃鈺呭传閸曨剙鈧洟姊?

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0110.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = learning



  right_portrait = scope:skonester_trait_edit_target
  immediate = {
    skonester_cheat_menu_sync_trait_edit_target_effect = yes
  }

  option = { name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_yannian_1 custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_yannian_1_tt trigger = { NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled } } scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = { if = { limit = { has_character_flag = skonester_yannian_dan_active } remove_character_flag = skonester_yannian_dan_active if = { limit = { has_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_original_age } set_age = var:skonester_yannian_dan_original_age } if = { limit = { has_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_original_age } remove_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_original_age } if = { limit = { has_trait = skonester_trait_yannian_dan } remove_trait = skonester_trait_yannian_dan } } else = { add_character_flag = skonester_yannian_dan_active if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_original_age } } set_variable = { name = skonester_yannian_dan_original_age value = age } } set_age = 25 if = { limit = { has_trait = incapable } remove_trait = incapable } add_trait = skonester_trait_yannian_dan } } skonester_traits_authorize_recipient_if_player_actor_effect = yes scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0111 }}

  option = { name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_yannian_2 custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_yannian_2_tt trigger = { NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled } } scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = { if = { limit = { has_character_flag = skonester_yannian_dan_mid_active } remove_character_flag = skonester_yannian_dan_mid_active if = { limit = { has_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_mid_original_age } set_age = var:skonester_yannian_dan_mid_original_age } if = { limit = { has_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_mid_original_age } remove_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_mid_original_age } if = { limit = { has_trait = skonester_trait_yannian_dan_mid } remove_trait = skonester_trait_yannian_dan_mid } } else = { add_character_flag = skonester_yannian_dan_mid_active if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_mid_original_age } } set_variable = { name = skonester_yannian_dan_mid_original_age value = age } } set_age = 25 if = { limit = { has_trait = incapable } remove_trait = incapable } add_trait = skonester_trait_yannian_dan_mid } } skonester_traits_authorize_recipient_if_player_actor_effect = yes scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0111 }}

  option = { name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_yannian_3 custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_yannian_3_tt trigger = { NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled } } scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = { if = { limit = { has_character_flag = skonester_yannian_dan_best_active } remove_character_flag = skonester_yannian_dan_best_active if = { limit = { has_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_best_original_age } set_age = var:skonester_yannian_dan_best_original_age } if = { limit = { has_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_best_original_age } remove_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_best_original_age } if = { limit = { has_trait = skonester_trait_yannian_dan_best } remove_trait = skonester_trait_yannian_dan_best } } else = { add_character_flag = skonester_yannian_dan_best_active if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = skonester_yannian_dan_best_original_age } } set_variable = { name = skonester_yannian_dan_best_original_age value = age } } set_age = 25 if = { limit = { has_trait = incapable } remove_trait = incapable } add_trait = skonester_trait_yannian_dan_best } } skonester_traits_authorize_recipient_if_player_actor_effect = yes scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0111 }}



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_baidubuqin

    custom_tooltip = skonester_cheat_menu.0015.toggle_baidubuqin_tt

    trigger = { NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled } }

    scope:skonester_trait_edit_target = {

      if = {

        limit = { has_trait = skonester_trait_baidubuqin }

        skonester_trait_baidubuqin_revoke_effect = yes

        remove_trait = skonester_trait_baidubuqin

      }

      else = {

        skonester_cheat_menu_grant_cheat_trait_effect = { TRAIT = skonester_trait_baidubuqin }

# 闂傚倷鐒﹂惇褰掑垂婵犳艾绐楅柟鐗堟緲閸ㄥ倹鎱ㄥ鍡楀⒒闁搞倕鐗撻弻锝夊Χ鎼达紕浠╁┑鐐茬墑閸旀垿寮婚妸銉㈡婵炲棙鍔楀▓銈夋⒑缁嬫鍎滅紒鎻掓健閹噣宕滆鐎氭岸鏌ょ喊鍗炲幋闁稿鎹囬幃婊堟寠婢跺矈鏀ㄩ梻浣告啞閸旀洖顕ｉ崼鏇犲彆妞ゆ帒瀚悡蹇涙煕閳╁喚娈欓悗姘閹叉悂鎮ч崼鐔封偓鎰版煛鐏炶濮傛い銏＄懇閹瑩顢楅崒婊冪倞闂傚倷绀侀幖顐ょ矓閺夋嚚娲閵堝懐鍔﹀銈嗗坊閸嬫挾绱掓径瀣唉妞ゃ垺鐟╁畷鎺戭潩閻撳孩鐣煎┑锛勫仜椤戝懐鈧稈鏅犲鏌ュ箹娴ｅ湱鍘甸柣搴㈢⊕閿氬┑顔ㄥ懐纾奸梺顒€绉抽幉鐐殽閻愨晛浜鹃柣鐔哥矊缁夌懓鐣峰ú顏勫耿婵☆垰鐨烽弸鏍⒑闁偛鑻晶瀵糕偓娈垮枛閻栧ジ寮幘缁樺亹妞ゆ劦婢€婢规洘绻濋姀锝呯厫闁告梹鍨块崺鈧い鎺嗗亾缂佸鎳撻悾鐑芥倻閽樺）鈺冩喐鎼达絿鐜绘俊銈呮噺閻撴稓鈧箍鍎遍幊蹇曠矚閸ф鈷掑┑鐐靛亾濞呭棛绱掔€ｎ亶妲圭紒缁樼箞瀹曟帒顫濋幍浣镐壕濠靛倸鎲￠悡娆愵殽閻愯尙浠㈤柣蹇ｅ櫍閺?婵?

skonester_cheat_menu.0155 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0155.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = default



  immediate = {

    skonester_char_gen_sync_recipient_to_scope_effect = yes

  }



  right_portrait = scope:skonester_char_gen_target



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0155.opt_gender_dyn

    scope:skonester_char_gen_target = {

      if = {

        limit = {

          OR = {

            NOT = { has_variable = skonester_spawn_talent_female_chance }

            var:skonester_spawn_talent_female_chance < 50

          }

        }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_spawn_talent_female_chance value = 100 }

      }

      else = {

        set_variable = { name = skonester_spawn_talent_female_chance value = 0 }

      }

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0155 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0155.military

    scope:skonester_char_gen_target = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_spawn_talent_employer

      skonester_cheat_menu_spawn_talent_military_effect = yes

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0155 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0155.stewardship

    scope:skonester_char_gen_target = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_spawn_talent_employer

      skonester_cheat_menu_spawn_talent_stewardship_effect = yes

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0155 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0155.diplomacy

    scope:skonester_char_gen_target = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_spawn_talent_employer

      skonester_cheat_menu_spawn_talent_diplomacy_effect = yes

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0155 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0155.intrigue

    scope:skonester_char_gen_target = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_spawn_talent_employer

      skonester_cheat_menu_spawn_talent_intrigue_effect = yes

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0155 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.back

    scope:actor = {

      trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0245

    }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.close

    skonester_char_gen_clear_recipient_global_effect = yes

  }

}



# 佳丽帅哥：文化菜单为 skonester_beauty_companion_classified_generated（3500+ 地理分层）；本页切换性别；「生成」再创建廷臣

skonester_cheat_menu.0180 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0180.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = default



  immediate = {

    skonester_char_gen_sync_recipient_to_scope_effect = yes

    scope:skonester_char_gen_target = {

      if = {

        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = skonester_companion_stored_culture } }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_companion_stored_culture value = scope:skonester_char_gen_target.culture }

      }

      if = {

        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = skonester_companion_female_chance } }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_companion_female_chance value = 100 }

      }

    }

  }



  right_portrait = scope:skonester_char_gen_target



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0180.opt_culture_dyn

    scope:skonester_char_gen_target = { add_character_flag = skonester_companion_picking_culture }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.3500 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0180.opt_gender_dyn

    scope:skonester_char_gen_target = {

      if = {

        limit = {

          OR = {

            NOT = { has_variable = skonester_companion_female_chance }

            var:skonester_companion_female_chance >= 50

          }

        }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_companion_female_chance value = 0 }

      }

      else = {

        set_variable = { name = skonester_companion_female_chance value = 100 }

      }

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0180 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0180.opt_spawn_now

    trigger = {

      scope:skonester_char_gen_target = { has_variable = skonester_companion_stored_culture }

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_spawn_companion_from_hub_prefs_effect = yes

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0180 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.back

    scope:actor = {

      trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0245

    }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.close

    skonester_char_gen_clear_recipient_global_effect = yes

  }

}



# 原版文化侧栏 · 模组快捷条：与本体窗口同开，可反复进入支柱/传统（不限次数）；0180 已用于佳丽帅哥故用 0181

    root = {

      skonester_open_selected_culture_vanilla_window_effect = yes

      trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0181

    }

  }

    root = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0150 }

    root = {

      add_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_0120

    }

  }

    root = {

      add_character_flag = skonester_culture_global_edit_active

      add_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_global_selected

      trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0171

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.close

    root = {

      remove_character_flag = skonester_culture_vanilla_edit_toolbar

    }

  }

}

  # 闂佽娴烽幊鎾诲箟闄囬妵鎰板礃椤帟鈧寧銇勮箛鎾村櫣妞ゆ洟浜堕弻锝呂熼崫鍕瘣濡炪們鍎抽崑鐐垫崲濞戙垹绾ч柟瀵稿Х椤旀垵顪冮妶搴′簻缂佸鎳撻悾鐑藉箮閽樺）鈺呮煏閸繄澧曟鐐╁亾闂傚倸鍊风欢锟犲磻閸曨垁鍥х暦閸ャ劌搴婇梺鍛婂姦閸犳牕螞濮椻偓閺屾盯濡烽幋婵嗘灓闁搞倖鐟╁娲传閸曨偅娈哄銈嗘⒐閸旀危閹邦厼顕遍柡澶嬪灩椤撳ジ姊虹憴鍕姢缁剧虎鍘炬竟鏇犵磼濡湱绠氬銈嗙墦閸婃澹曠捄銊х＜妞ゆ棁鍋愭牎缂備礁顑呴ˇ鐢搞€佸鈧幃銏℃姜閻楀牊绶氶梻鍌欑劍鐎笛呯矙閹达附鍋嬪┑鐘叉搐閺嬩焦銇勯弴妤€浜鹃悗?婵犵數鍋熼ˉ鎰板磻閹邦厽鍙忓瀣閸?闂傚倷绀佸﹢閬嶁€﹂崼銉嬪洭寮跺▎鐐瘜闂佺鏈粙鎾汇€呴悜鑺ョ叆婵犻潧妫楅顐ょ磼鐎ｎ亷鍔熼柍褜鍓氶鏍窗閺嶎厽鏅濇い蹇撳閻掕棄鈹戦悩瀹犲婵☆偅锕㈤弻鐔封枔閸喗鐏撻梺鍦帶閵堟悂寮婚妸銉㈡婵妫欓鍡欑磽娴ｄ粙鍝虹紒璇插暣閳ユ棃鍩€椤掍椒绻嗘い鏍ㄤ緱閸庛儵鏌涘顒佸缂佺粯绋撶槐娆撴偐閸濆嫬甯梻浣哥枃椤曆囧Χ缁嬭法鏆﹂柨婵嗩樈閺佸倿鏌涢弴銊ュ箻闁?vanilla

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0130.open_mod_hub_selected_culture

    trigger = { exists = scope:skonester_selected_culture }

    scope:actor = {

      add_character_flag = skonester_culture_global_edit_active

      add_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_global_selected

      trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0171

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0130.back_to_editor

    trigger = {

      scope:actor = {

        OR = {

          has_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_0120

          has_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_global_selected

          has_character_flag = skonester_quick_modifications_innovation_entry

        }

      }

    }

    scope:actor = {

      if = {

        limit = { has_character_flag = skonester_quick_modifications_innovation_entry }

        remove_character_flag = skonester_quick_modifications_innovation_entry

        trigger_event = skonester_quick_modifications.1000

      }

      else_if = {

        limit = { has_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_global_selected }

        remove_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_global_selected

        trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0171

      }

      else_if = {

        limit = { has_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_0120 }

        remove_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_0120

        if = {

          limit = { has_character_flag = skonester_culture_vanilla_edit_toolbar }

          trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0181

        }

        else = {

        skonester_cheat_menu_culture_editor_hub_return_effect = yes

        }

      }

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0130.close

    trigger = { scope:actor = { has_character_flag = skonester_quick_modifications_innovation_entry } }

    scope:actor = { skonester_quick_modifications_innovation_close_effect = yes }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0130.close

    trigger = {
      scope:actor = {
        NOR = {
          has_character_flag = skonester_culture_innov_from_0120
          has_character_flag = skonester_quick_modifications_innovation_entry
        }
      }
    }

  }

}

# 闂傚倷绀侀幉锟犳偡閿旂晫绠惧┑鐘叉搐閺嬩焦銇勯幘鍗炵仼鏉╂繈鏌ｈ箛鏇炰沪鐎规洘顭堝Λ銏ゆ⒒娴ｅ湱婀介柛銊ユ惈鍗遍柟鐑樻磸娴滃湱鈧箍鍎遍ˇ顖滄兜閳ь剟姊洪崫鍕閼裤倝鏌涚€ｎ偅灏い鎾瑰缁犳盯寮撮悩闈涚倞闂備浇顕х€涒晝绮欓幒鎳ㄦ椽寮介婧惧亾閸涙惌鏁嗛柛灞剧矆缁楀姊洪幐搴ｂ槈閻庢凹鍙冨畷?闂?闂傚倷鑳堕崢褔宕幐搴闁归棿绀佺粈澶屾喐閻楀牆绗掔紒鐘冲灩閹茬顓兼径濠冩К闂佺硶鍓濈粙鎴ｇ箽濠电偠鎻徊鍧椻€﹂崼銉﹀珔闁绘柨鍚嬮悡鐘碘偓瑙勬礀濞层倖绂掗埡鍛厱閹兼番鍨婚幊鍥р攽閳ュ磭鎽犻柟宄版嚇瀹曨偊濡烽‖顕呬邯濮婃椽宕崟顐ｆ闂佺閰ｆ禍璺虹暦鐟欏嫭鍎熼柕蹇嬪灮椤旀洟姊哄畷鍥ㄥ殌缂併劑浜跺畷鎴﹀箻鐎涙ê顎撻柣鐔哥懃鐎氬憡顨ヨ箛娑欌拺婵懓娲ら崝婊呯磼鐎ｎ偄绗х紒顔硷躬閺佸倿鎮惧畝濠冮敜闁荤喐绮嶇换鍕ｉ幇顑芥瀻闁规儳纾悡鎴︽⒑缁嬫寧婀扮紒娲畺瀹?2859903263 闂傚倷鐒﹂惇褰掑礉瀹€鈧埀顒佸嚬閸撶喖骞冮崸妤€绀嬫い鏍ㄧ☉娴犮垽鏌ｉ姀鈺佺伇闁哥姵姘ㄧ划?

skonester_cheat_menu.0001 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0001.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = pregnancy

  immediate = {
    skonester_cheat_menu_sync_pregnancy_target_effect = yes
  }

  right_portrait = scope:skonester_pregnancy_target

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0001.female

    trigger = {
      exists = scope:skonester_pregnancy_target
    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {
        has_trait = pregnant
      }
    }

    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {

      if = {

        limit = {

          has_trait = pregnant

        }

        set_pregnancy_gender = female

        remove_character_flag = skonester_unborn_male

        add_character_flag = skonester_unborn_female

      }

      remove_character_flag = skonester_bgp_pending

    }

    scope:actor = {
      trigger_event = {
        id = skonester_cheat_menu.0001
        days = 0
      }
    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0001.male

    trigger = {
      exists = scope:skonester_pregnancy_target
    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {
        has_trait = pregnant
      }
    }

    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {

      if = {

        limit = {

          has_trait = pregnant

        }

        set_pregnancy_gender = male

        remove_character_flag = skonester_unborn_female

        add_character_flag = skonester_unborn_male

      }

      remove_character_flag = skonester_bgp_pending

    }

    scope:actor = {
      trigger_event = {
        id = skonester_cheat_menu.0001
        days = 0
      }
    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0001.twins

    trigger = {
      exists = scope:skonester_pregnancy_target
    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {
        has_trait = pregnant
      }
    }

    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {

      if = {

        limit = {

          has_trait = pregnant

        }

        set_num_pregnancy_children = 2

        add_character_flag = skonester_expecting_twins

      }

      remove_character_flag = skonester_bgp_pending

    }

    scope:actor = {
      trigger_event = {
        id = skonester_cheat_menu.0001
        days = 0
      }
    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0001.deliver_today

    trigger = {
      exists = scope:skonester_pregnancy_target
    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {
        has_trait = pregnant
      }
    }

    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {

      skonester_cheat_menu_instant_deliver_today_effect = yes

      remove_character_flag = skonester_bgp_pending

    }

    scope:actor = {
      trigger_event = {
        id = skonester_cheat_menu.0001
        days = 0
      }
    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0001.abort

    trigger = {
      exists = scope:skonester_pregnancy_target
    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {
        OR = {
          has_trait = pregnant
          is_pregnant = yes
        }
      }
    }

    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = {

      end_pregnancy = yes

      pregnancy_cleanup_effect = yes

      remove_character_flag = skonester_expecting_twins

      remove_character_flag = skonester_unborn_female

      remove_character_flag = skonester_unborn_male

      remove_character_flag = skonester_bgp_pending

    }

    scope:actor = {
      trigger_event = {
        id = skonester_cheat_menu.0001
        days = 0
      }
    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.back

    scope:skonester_pregnancy_target = { remove_character_flag = skonester_bgp_pending }

    scope:actor = { skonester_cheat_menu_open_main_hub_window_effect = yes }

  }

}

skonester_cheat_menu.0002 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0002.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = intrigue

  immediate = {

    skonester_cheat_menu_sync_target_effect = yes

  }





  right_portrait = scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0002.beheaded

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      death = {

        killer = scope:actor

        death_reason = death_decapitated

      }

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0002.disease

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      death = {

        death_reason = death_typhus

      }

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0002.natural

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      death = {

        death_reason = death_natural_causes

      }

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0002.cancel

    skonester_cheat_menu_clear_recipient_effect = yes

  }

}



# 右键「Skonester菜单」：灭族（清空目标 AI 的全族）



# --- restored from desktop reference ---

skonester_cheat_menu.0003 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0003.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = intrigue

  immediate = {

    skonester_cheat_menu_sync_target_effect = yes

  }





  right_portrait = scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0003.execute

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      dynasty = {

        every_dynasty_member = {

          limit = {

            is_alive = yes

            is_ai = yes

            NOT = { this = scope:actor }

          }

          death = {

            death_reason = death_natural_causes

          }

        }

      }

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0003.cancel

    skonester_cheat_menu_clear_recipient_effect = yes

  }

}



# 右键「Skonester菜单」：年龄修改（+10/+20/+30 岁；−10/−20/−30 岁，最低 0 岁）。set_age 不重算年龄相关健康，故另用变量 + 永久 modifier 同步健康条（见 skonester_cheat_menu_age_edit_health_effects）。



# --- restored from desktop reference ---

skonester_cheat_menu.0075 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0075.t

  desc = skonester_cheat_menu.0075.desc

  theme = default

  immediate = {

    skonester_cheat_menu_sync_target_effect = yes

  }





  right_portrait = scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0075.plus_10

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      set_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu value = age }

      change_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu add = 10 }

      set_age = var:skonester_tmp_age_menu

      remove_variable = skonester_tmp_age_menu

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_age_edit_after_plus_10_effect = yes

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0075 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0075.plus_20

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      set_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu value = age }

      change_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu add = 20 }

      set_age = var:skonester_tmp_age_menu

      remove_variable = skonester_tmp_age_menu

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_age_edit_after_plus_20_effect = yes

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0075 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0075.plus_30

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      set_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu value = age }

      change_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu add = 30 }

      set_age = var:skonester_tmp_age_menu

      remove_variable = skonester_tmp_age_menu

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_age_edit_after_plus_30_effect = yes

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0075 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0075.minus_10

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      set_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu value = age }

      change_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu add = -10 }

      if = {

        limit = { var:skonester_tmp_age_menu < 0 }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu value = 0 }

      }

      set_age = var:skonester_tmp_age_menu

      remove_variable = skonester_tmp_age_menu

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_age_edit_after_minus_10_effect = yes

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0075 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0075.minus_20

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      set_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu value = age }

      change_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu add = -20 }

      if = {

        limit = { var:skonester_tmp_age_menu < 0 }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu value = 0 }

      }

      set_age = var:skonester_tmp_age_menu

      remove_variable = skonester_tmp_age_menu

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_age_edit_after_minus_20_effect = yes

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0075 }

  }

  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0075.minus_30

    scope:skonester_cheat_menu_target = {

      set_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu value = age }

      change_variable = { name = skonester_tmp_age_menu add = -30 }

      if = {

    }

    skonester_cheat_menu_clear_recipient_effect = yes

  }

# 右键「Skonester菜单」：增加优秀身体特质（倾城/多产/强壮等）



# --- restored from desktop reference ---

skonester_cheat_menu.0004 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0004.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = default



  right_portrait = scope:recipient



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0004.beauty

    scope:recipient = {

      if = { limit = { has_trait = beauty_bad_1 } remove_trait = beauty_bad_1 }

      if = { limit = { has_trait = beauty_bad_2 } remove_trait = beauty_bad_2 }

      if = { limit = { has_trait = beauty_bad_3 } remove_trait = beauty_bad_3 }

      if = { limit = { has_trait = beauty_good_1 } remove_trait = beauty_good_1 }

      if = { limit = { has_trait = beauty_good_2 } remove_trait = beauty_good_2 }

      add_trait = beauty_good_3

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0004 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0004.fecund

    scope:recipient = {

      add_trait = fecund

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0004 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0004.physique

    scope:recipient = {

      if = { limit = { has_trait = physique_bad_1 } remove_trait = physique_bad_1 }

      if = { limit = { has_trait = physique_bad_2 } remove_trait = physique_bad_2 }

      if = { limit = { has_trait = physique_bad_3 } remove_trait = physique_bad_3 }

      if = { limit = { has_trait = weak } remove_trait = weak }

      add_trait = physique_good_3

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0004 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0004.strong

    scope:recipient = {

      if = { limit = { has_trait = weak } remove_trait = weak }

      add_trait = strong

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0004 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0004.cancel

  }

}



## 右键「Skonester菜单」：爱爱（两阶段：闺房互动 → 事后；怀孕在确认后进行，不再显示「已怀孕」字样）



# --- restored from desktop reference ---

skonester_cheat_menu.0010 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0010.t

  desc = skonester_cheat_menu.0010.desc

  theme = romance

  override_background = { reference = bedchamber }



  left_portrait = {

    character = scope:actor

    animation = flirtation

  }

  right_portrait = {

    character = scope:recipient

    animation = flirtation_left

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0010.continue

    hidden_effect = {

      scope:recipient = {

        if = {

          limit = {

            is_female = yes

            is_pregnant = no

            is_adult = yes

            fertility > 0

          }

          if = {

            limit = {

              skonester_stealth_cheat_pregnancy_trigger = yes

            }

            # 非配偶/情人/妾等：隐匿怀孕（unknown bastard）；有主配偶则假定父亲为其，否则假定仍为玩家。

            make_pregnant = {

              father = scope:actor

              known_bastard = no

            }

            if = {

              limit = {

                exists = primary_spouse

              }

              primary_spouse = {

                save_scope_as = skonester_stealth_assumed_father

              }

              set_pregnancy_assumed_father = scope:skonester_stealth_assumed_father

              clear_saved_scope = skonester_stealth_assumed_father

            }

            else = {

              set_pregnancy_assumed_father = scope:actor

            }

          }

          else = {

            # 配偶/情人/灵魂伴侣/actor 之妾：假定父亲 = 玩家（与旧版一致）。

            make_pregnant = {

              father = scope:actor

            }

            set_pregnancy_assumed_father = scope:actor

          }

        }

      }

      scope:actor = { add_character_flag = is_naked }

      scope:recipient = { add_character_flag = is_naked }

    }

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0012 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0010.cancel

  }

}



# --- restored from desktop reference ---

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0012.t

  desc = skonester_cheat_menu.0012.desc

    character = scope:actor

  }

    character = scope:recipient

    animation = ecstasy

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0012.ok

    scope:actor = {

      if = { limit = { has_character_flag = is_naked } remove_character_flag = is_naked }

    }

    scope:recipient = {

      if = { limit = { has_character_flag = is_naked } remove_character_flag = is_naked }

    }

  }

}

skonester_cheat_menu.0021 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0021.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = default



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.back

    trigger = { scope:actor = { has_character_flag = skonester_culture_quick_from_0120 } }

    scope:actor = {

      remove_character_flag = skonester_culture_quick_from_0120

      skonester_cheat_menu_culture_editor_hub_return_effect = yes

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0021.browse_all_cultures

    scope:actor = {

      add_character_flag = skonester_culture_reform_pick

      trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.3020

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0021.pillars_menu

    scope:actor = {

      remove_character_flag = skonester_culture_hub_from_0120

      trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0040

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0021.traditions_menu

    scope:actor = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.0050 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0021.culture_tradition_editor

    root = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.5000 }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0021.hybrid_with_liege

    trigger = { exists = liege }

    scope:actor = { create_hybrid_culture_with_side_effects = liege.culture }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0021.random_add_1

    scope:actor = { culture = { add_random_valid_tradition_replace_if_necessary = scope:actor } }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0021.random_remove_1

    scope:actor = { culture = { remove_random_culture_tradition = yes } }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0021.close

    scope:actor = {

      remove_character_flag = skonester_culture_reform_pick

    }

  }

## 濠电姷鏁搁崑鐐哄箰閹间礁绠犳俊顖濄€€閺嬪秹骞栧ǎ顒€濡肩紒鐘劦閺屽秷顧侀柛鎾跺枎椤曪綁宕归銏㈢獮闂佸綊鍋婇崢鍝勵潩閿曞倹鈷?

skonester_cheat_menu.5040 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.5040.t

  desc = skonester_cheat_menu.5040.desc

  theme = learning



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.5040.confirm

    if = {

      limit = { exists = scope:skonester_selected_culture }

      scope:skonester_selected_culture = {
        save_scope_as = skonester_clear_traditions_culture
        every_culture_tradition = {
          save_scope_as = skonester_clear_tradition
          scope:skonester_clear_traditions_culture = {
            remove_culture_tradition = scope:skonester_clear_tradition
          }
        }

      }

    }

    else = {

      root.culture = {
        save_scope_as = skonester_clear_traditions_culture
        every_culture_tradition = {
          save_scope_as = skonester_clear_tradition
          scope:skonester_clear_traditions_culture = {
            remove_culture_tradition = scope:skonester_clear_tradition
          }
        }

      }

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.5040.back

    root = { trigger_event = skonester_cheat_menu.5001 }

  }



  option = { name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.close }

}

skonester_cheat_menu.0190 = {

  type = character_event

  title = skonester_cheat_menu.0190.t

  desc = skonester_ui_no_hover_tooltip

  theme = romance



  right_portrait = scope:skonester_force_betrothal_recipient



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0190.force_betrothal

    trigger = { NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled } }

    scope:skonester_force_betrothal_actor = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_actor

    }

    scope:skonester_force_betrothal_recipient = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_recipient

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_target

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_target_save

      if = {

        limit = { exists = faith }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_original_faith value = scope:skonester_betrothal_target.faith }

      }

      if = {

        limit = { exists = culture }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_original_culture value = scope:skonester_betrothal_target.culture }

      }

      while = {

        limit = { exists = primary_spouse }

        divorce = primary_spouse

      }

      if = {

        limit = { exists = betrothed }

        break_betrothal = betrothed

      }

      if = {

        limit = { is_concubine = yes }

        remove_concubine = yes

      }

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_actor = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_actor_save

      if = {

        limit = { exists = faith }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_actor_original_faith value = scope:skonester_betrothal_actor.faith }

      }

      if = {

        limit = { exists = culture }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_actor_original_culture value = scope:skonester_betrothal_actor.culture }

      }

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_target_save = {

      set_character_faith = faith:skonester_force_marry_faith

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_actor_save = {

      set_character_faith = faith:skonester_force_marry_faith

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_actor_save = {
      create_betrothal = scope:skonester_betrothal_target_save

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_actor_save = {

      if = {

        limit = { has_variable = skonester_actor_original_faith }

        set_character_faith = var:skonester_actor_original_faith

        remove_variable = skonester_actor_original_faith

      }

      if = {

        limit = { has_variable = skonester_actor_original_culture }

        set_culture = var:skonester_actor_original_culture

        remove_variable = skonester_actor_original_culture

      }

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_target_save = {

      if = {

        limit = { has_variable = skonester_original_faith }

        set_character_faith = var:skonester_original_faith

        remove_variable = skonester_original_faith

      }

      if = {

        limit = { has_variable = skonester_original_culture }

        set_culture = var:skonester_original_culture

        remove_variable = skonester_original_culture

      }

    }

  }



  option = {

    name = skonester_cheat_menu.0190.force_marriage

    trigger = { NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_cheat_menu_menu_disabled } }

    scope:skonester_force_betrothal_actor = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_actor

    }

    scope:skonester_force_betrothal_recipient = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_recipient

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_target

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_target_save

      if = {

        limit = { exists = faith }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_original_faith value = scope:skonester_betrothal_target.faith }

      }

      if = {

        limit = { exists = culture }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_original_culture value = scope:skonester_betrothal_target.culture }

      }

      while = {

        limit = { exists = primary_spouse }

        divorce = primary_spouse

      }

      if = {

        limit = { exists = betrothed }

        break_betrothal = betrothed

      }

      if = {

        limit = { is_concubine = yes }

        remove_concubine = yes

      }

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_actor = {

      save_scope_as = skonester_betrothal_actor_save

      if = {

        limit = { exists = faith }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_actor_original_faith value = scope:skonester_betrothal_actor.faith }

      }

      if = {

        limit = { exists = culture }

        set_variable = { name = skonester_actor_original_culture value = scope:skonester_betrothal_actor.culture }

      }

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_target_save = {

      set_character_faith = faith:skonester_force_marry_faith

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_actor_save = {

      set_character_faith = faith:skonester_force_marry_faith

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_actor_save = {

      marry = scope:skonester_betrothal_target_save

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_actor_save = {

      if = {

        limit = { has_variable = skonester_actor_original_faith }

        set_character_faith = var:skonester_actor_original_faith

        remove_variable = skonester_actor_original_faith

      }

      if = {

        limit = { has_variable = skonester_actor_original_culture }

        set_culture = var:skonester_actor_original_culture

        remove_variable = skonester_actor_original_culture

      }

    }

    scope:skonester_betrothal_target_save = {

      if = {

        limit = { has_variable = skonester_original_faith }

        set_character_faith = var:skonester_original_faith

        remove_variable = skonester_original_faith

      }

      if = {

        limit = { has_variable = skonester_original_culture }

        set_culture = var:skonester_original_culture

        remove_variable = skonester_original_culture

      }

    }

  }



  option = { name = skonester_cheat_menu.00x.close }

}
